## Nov 18 — SECOND G7-10 read-lock fix: protect SQL receipt hash from concurrent writers; actual Windows CI PASS

After the accepted photo-envelope read-lock fix, the existing owner-local read-only backup cadence script had the parallel **SQL file read race**: receipt-length metadata and `Get-FileHash` were separate and a writer could interfere with the inspected bytes; IO exceptions could expose owner filesystem paths. Actual source [e325bce](https://github.com/kylebrooks86/first-in-response-website/commit/e325bce59881e7da2f617e27285b7803156fd5bd), PowerShell 5.1-compatible expression [2efeb16](https://github.com/kylebrooks86/first-in-response-website/commit/2efeb160ec7f0259f6133a04238b6d742fb010bf): locks SQL read handle with `FileShare.Read`, measures stream length and hashes **that same locked stream** via SHA256, compares both to local receipt, and sanitizes read failures. New actual Windows child-process regression [43a80bd](https://github.com/kylebrooks86/first-in-response-website/commit/43a80bdf6da3a8506f5403f5fb477bf525120c35) injects active SQL writer and requires no false receipt PASS or private path leak. ## Nov 18 — NEW G7-10 encrypted photo archive concurrent-writer safety fix + real Windows CI PASS

Actual source weakness in the existing owner-only offline `release/owner_backup_cadence_review.ps1`: it read photo archive bytes with `FileShare.ReadWrite` and compared against file length sampled before the open, so a concurrent writer could change file data during an apparent structure check; raw open errors risked echoing private Windows paths. [Source fix 75f00c6](https://github.com/kylebrooks86/first-in-response-website/commit/75f00c6c9491a45cce2b0afc9921bd5ce986626e) now opens `FileShare.Read`, checks archive length/header from the **same protected stream**, and returns sanitized `PHOTO_ENVELOPE_READ_UNAVAILABLE` for file access failures. [Focused test b8d4f23](https://github.com/kylebrooks86/first-in-response-website/commit/b8d4f234cab8e54bd1d9c23ab3d4ada60afdf4fd) reproduces an **actual concurrent writer in a second Windows PowerShell process** with synthetic files, requiring a refused inspection and no private path leak.

## November 18 — G7-10 cadence false-freshness defect FIXED and real Windows CI PASS (source only)

In existing **draft PR270**, the read-only owner backup reviewer previously treated the local `LastWriteTimeUtc` of SQL and encrypted photo copies as backup freshness. Downloading or copying old backup files resets their local timestamps and could wrongly print `WITHIN_LOCAL_AGE_WINDOW`. **Code fix [7036190](https://github.com/kylebrooks86/first-in-response-website/commit/703619004eec6a2f42397c5b4f405c09ff5653be)** now reports local file age as such, returns `CADENCE=CAPTURE_AGE_UNVERIFIED` for files with recent local timestamps, and refuses to claim that the backup capture date was authenticated; truly old local timestamps still conservatively recommend a new backup. **Regression [feb02b8](https://github.com/kylebrooks86/first-in-response-website/commit/feb02b87a026cd5871d98f5c766e4d05d60d8181)** covers a copied old backup with freshly reset Windows timestamps; runbook updated [3673eba](https://github.com/kylebrooks86/first-in-response-website/commit/3673eba5ec56965007453d05895fe0a7e3ae6c16).

**Actual Windows PowerShell CI:** [run #44441724730](https://github.com/kylebrooks86/first-in-response-website/actions/runs/44441724730) **COMPLETED SUCCESS**, real Windows job #135283924575 and the offline fail-closed test step SUCCESS. [Public website verify #44441724469](https://github.com/kylebrooks86/first-in-response-website/actions/runs/44441724469) SUCCESS. This is an actual code fix, **not** verification of newly captured owner records/photos, atomic snapshot, full future-data rollback, or Gate7 acceptance. **G7-10 remains [ ]; 50/95 checked, 45 OPEN; formal G1/G2/G3/G8 4/10; NEW LIVE DARK/NO-GO, Job #0001 unissued.** No Cloudflare login, subscriptions/R2, provider/SQL/Stripe writes, deployment, merge or new owner test.

## Nov 17 — customer appointment messages cannot confirm past jobs (REAL SOURCE FIX; NO DEPLOY)

On existing **DRAFT PR #270**, corrected the actual `customerAppointmentTemplateReadiness` guard ([logic `1b95940`](https://github.com/kylebrooks86/first-in-response-website/commit/1b9594071e317b29f2e9f038827554c3c5e94d1b)): a saved job still marked **scheduled** with its time already elapsed could previously pass template readiness and prepare a misleading appointment confirmation/reminder. The guard now requires a valid **future** scheduled time and a valid clock for any saved template containing `@schedule_date`; explicitly directs the owner to verify stale job status. Nonappointment custom messages remain available. [Actual MessageComposer click-time fix `0a1ebce`](https://github.com/kylebrooks86/first-in-response-website/commit/0a1ebce4d30d935227c7710990fea74824e20217) revalidates immediately before the durable prepared-message POST even if the modal was opened before the appointment elapsed. No automatic customer communication, money, or database write in this change.

[Targeted regression `c78a172`](https://github.com/kylebrooks86/first-in-response-website/commit/c78a1728e987f3a7ca668cbede238117a0112e92) adds seven real-helper schedule boundary and compatibility assertions plus the actual composer source click-time contract. [Mandatory staging wiring `14b9333`](https://github.com/kylebrooks86/first-in-response-website/commit/14b93339702f9665b7bf799d3af62905153f7222) now **executes** the formerly uninvoked `scripts/test-customer-contact-ready-actions.mjs` in the existing staging source/customer regression step. [Exact-head staging run #44195433408](https://github.com/kylebrooks86/first-in-response-website/actions/runs/44195433408) was **PENDING** at first readback; do not claim success or the full matrix yet. Any later green outcome must be recorded separately on precise SHA/step.

**Acceptance boundary:** This is an actual source and CI-enforcement correction, **unmerged/undeployed** and not a physical hosted, customer-send, or live environment gate. **45/95 accepted, 50 remain, formal 4/10 G1/G2/G3/G8, G4/G5/G6/G7/G9/G10 OPEN, NEW LIVE DARK/NO-GO, first genuine Job #0001 unused.** Do not redo existing iPhone/Acer/backup tests, attempt Cloudflare Work login, R2/paid subscriptions, create tokens, provider POST, Access/routing, Stripe/money, SQL/D1 writes, merge or production cutover without separate exact approval.

### G9-02 historical TEST upload failure disposition

- **Inactive version HTTP 400 / 10162:** the [actual provider error](https://github.com/kylebrooks86/first-in-response-website/issues/19#issuecomment-6785132652) was an unsupported module content type per [Cloudflare's error documentation](https://developers.cloudflare.com/workers/observability/errors/). The envelope used `application/json` for three generated Vinext metadata modules. The reviewed correction changed those bytes' MIME to `text/plain`, a [supported version-upload module type](https://developers.cloudflare.com/api/resources/workers/subresources/scripts/subresources/versions/methods/create/). The [accepted source](<https://github.com/kylebrooks86/first-in-response-website/blob/a655ecbd77769add27d9ed6c5bc86c3376723563/business-app-parity/cloudflare-launch/stripe-test-worker/gate9_offline_version_envelope.mjs>) retains the correction, and a separate historical approval produced a [successful inactive version and independent 0% readback](https://github.com/kylebrooks86/first-in-response-website/issues/19#issuecomment-6786144101). Exact offending file was never returned by the provider.
- **Asset registration HTTP 500 / 10013:** the original incident was [tracked in the provider project](https://github.com/kylebrooks86/first-in-response-website/issues/19#issuecomment-6840724274). The separately sealed Worker-only fallback embeds the 41 assets as Data modules with no ASSETS binding or assets-upload-session, and [the exact saved fallback packaged and booted offline with all seven inactive denial probes passing](https://github.com/kylebrooks86/first-in-response-website/actions/runs/44086158101). FIRE's source disposition is a verified bypass. A provider-side fix to the asset registration endpoint has not been established.
- Both legacy execution entrypoints are preflight/review-only ([Node](https://github.com/kylebrooks86/first-in-response-website/commit/2e7fd8124dcb4147ebcc0aca89bbaa4d175e9f47), [PowerShell](https://github.com/kylebrooks86/first-in-response-website/commit/fcd30f96483e5136253a428e978708fef0b00c2d)). The original approvals and attempt locks remain spent. New TEST upload authorization, target-bound preflight, actual 0% hosting proof for the new candidate and signed Stripe TEST delivery remain G9-03 onward.

The October ZIP does not, by itself, prove a **current complete** target-bound original-photo inventory, same-snapshot encrypted D1+photo pair, independent owner-held encrypted records file or per-photo job association. Therefore retain G7-03/04/07 as open for only those genuinely additional requirements, and credit the original device recovery within each item's evidence scope. Separately, the [November 15 private Drive inventory](https://github.com/kylebrooks86/first-in-response-website/issues/19#issuecomment-6902716921) found 31 backup-folder entries but no `.firerecords`, `.firephotos`, or `.firepair` filename extensions in that particular folder; this does not establish those files are absent elsewhere. The complete owner D1+photo pairing GUI was [source-built but default hidden](https://github.com/kylebrooks86/first-in-response-website/issues/19#issuecomment-6908634965), so do not confuse that new unreleased feature with the earlier successful deployed QA photo ZIP restore. No new owner screenshot, Cloudflare sign-in or restore is needed for the already accepted October evidence.

### Remaining evidence after the test audit

- **G7-07:** The [actual paired recovery test](https://github.com/kylebrooks86/first-in-response-website/actions/runs/43798962371) and [native SQLite recovery test](https://github.com/kylebrooks86/first-in-response-website/actions/runs/43664232024) restored and reopened the synthetic source-photo ZIP, rechecked original bytes/CRC/SHA and preserved photo-to-customer identity. The [photo archive record](https://github.com/kylebrooks86/first-in-response-website/blob/4343e345b697dc95db2e92dc4b89359a3fcddd8d/business-app-parity/shared/lib/offline-photo-backup.ts) carries `customerId` and category but no job identifier. The [device vault](https://github.com/kylebrooks86/first-in-response-website/blob/4343e345b697dc95db2e92dc4b89359a3fcddd8d/business-app-parity/shared/lib/local-photo-vault.ts) indexes by customer. A numbered job was also restored in the DB fixture; that does not prove any individual photo's association with that job. Require verifiable job-level association where applicable and the actual owner backup before closing this item.
- **G6-08:** Prior executed [built Worker association and synthetic origin controls](https://github.com/kylebrooks86/first-in-response-website/issues/19#issuecomment-6722872604) and [shared-app QA native SQLite checks](https://github.com/kylebrooks86/first-in-response-website/actions/runs/44024946759) already cover local cross-origin denial and no-customer-data/charge behavior. Keep those results. The remaining requirement is hosted mutation/privilege evidence for the intended candidate. A complete live customer separation test is independently G6-05.
- **G5-01:** The original [independent STAGING read-only query](https://github.com/kylebrooks86/first-in-response-website/issues/19#issuecomment-6513700760) returned 22 records but only its last three names were visible. The [older locally restored export](https://github.com/kylebrooks86/first-in-response-website/issues/19#issuecomment-6563777438) confirms record counts and integrity but did not record the complete ordered filenames. Reuse both. If that original full result is still available, read it. Otherwise the only journal capture needed is the ordered `id,name` output from the already verified `fire-app-staging-db` (physical D1 `afb2c05a-d794-4a9a-b580-924ce01c26ad`), through the owner's existing authenticated session. Do not collect business rows, repeat DR/NEW LIVE 26/26 proof or apply migrations.

SELECT id, name FROM d1_migrations ORDER BY id;
